Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a parabolic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 691. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 977/5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to parabolic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 691/977 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the parabolic structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 128. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 352 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 128 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the parabolic structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 352. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
